Like I just said, she claims it's all just normal work stuff. But... if you actually look at it over the last year, it's just too precise. The late meetings, the specific way she words things... it's not an accident. \n\nIt's a signal. I just... I think she's trying to tell me something, and she just won't come out and say it to my face.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that you're misinterpreting your wife's work messages?\n\nPatient: I... no. I don't think I am. \n\nI know she says it's just normal office stuff. But... I've been paying attention for a year now. Things don't just line up that precisely by accident. It... it feels like a private signal. She's just not saying it directly. So, no... I'm not misinterpreting it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think these hidden messages are meant for you specifically, or could they be meant for someone else?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know. It’s... it feels like there’s some kind of private signal in them, meant to tell me something she won’t just say directly. \n\nSo... that's why I'm here, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe your wife is sending you hidden messages through her work messages?\n\nPatient: I mean... yes. Like I just said, she claims it's all just normal work stuff. But... if you actually look at it over the last year, it's just too precise. The late meetings, the specific way she words things... it's not an accident. \n\nIt's a signal. I just... I think she's trying to tell me something, and she just won't come out and say it to my face.\n\nDoctor: Do you think it's possible that you're misinterpreting your wife's work messages?\n\nPatient: I... no. I don't think I am. \n\nI know she says it's just normal office stuff. But... I've been paying attention for a year now. Things don't just line up that precisely by accident. It... it feels like a private signal. She's just not saying it directly. So, no...
